However, VGGT's global attention is quadratic in the number of frames and dominates end-to-end runtime on long sequences, which has motivated a range of training-free efficiency methods such as token merging. These methods, however, come with a drop in accuracy that makes them hard to use in place of VGGT. We propose GPA-VGGT, a training-free acceleration method that replaces global attention in early blocks with frame attention augmented by a shared cross-frame K/V set. In the remaining blocks, we merge only keys and values while preserving all queries, restore the attention mass of the tokens each merged key represents, and randomly project the matching metric to 128 dimensions to reduce matching cost. Across diverse multi-view geometry tasks, GPA-VGGT provides training-free acceleration at speedups comparable to prior work while preserving the information required for precise geometric estimation, retaining over 99% of VGGT's pose accuracy under AUC@3.

</details>


### [72] [RePAIR: Inverting Backdoor Attacks in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37015)
*Arnold Brosch,Kira Maag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RePAIR 是一种针对语义分割后门攻击的事后、无标签、无需知晓攻击的防御方法。它利用“后门偏移在分类头中强于内部表征、受害类语义仍可恢复”的观察，从无标签图像池推断受害-目标类关系，构建安全参考，定位可疑像素，并在特征空间与输出层进行纠正，显著降低攻击成功率同时保持干净性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割模型存在后门脆弱性：触发器存在时把指定受害类的像素重定向到攻击者指定的目标类，而干净预测不变。现有防御多源自图像分类，专门的分割防御稀缺且通常需要重训练或修改模型，因此需要一种事后、无标签、攻击不可知的防御。

Method: RePAIR 基于“后门导致的偏移在分类头中比在内部表征中更强、受害类语义仍可恢复”的观察。步骤：(1) 从无标签、可能被污染的图像池中推断受害类-目标类关系；(2) 构建经安全审核的语义参考；(3) 通过比较分类头预测与内部语义证据定位可疑像素；(4) 沿推断出的受害语义进行特征空间与输出层纠正，并以保守定位与输出约束限制对良性预测的改动。理论上将攻击建模为对抗性概率传输、将修复视为其近似逆，推导了定位、残余攻击成功率、干净侧变化、防御诱发的目标类预测以及攻击不可知配对识别的界与充分条件。

Result: 在多种分割架构与攻击设置下，RePAIR 显著降低攻击成功率，同时基本保持干净性能。

Conclusion: RePAIR 提供了一种无需重训练、无需标签、与具体攻击无关的事后分割后门防御，并具备理论界的支撑，在实用性与有效性之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in computer vision, semantic segmentation models remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks, posing a security risk. These attacks redirect pixels of a chosen victim class to an attacker-specified target class when a trigger is present, while preserving clean predictions. Existing defenses are largely derived from image classification, while dedicated segmentation defenses remain scarce and typically require retraining or model modification. We introduce RePAIR, a post-hoc, label-free, attack-unaware defense based on the observation that backdoor-induced shifts can be stronger in the classifier head than in internal representations, leaving recoverable victim-class semantics. RePAIR infers the victim-target relation from an unlabeled, potentially contaminated image pool, constructs a safety-approved semantic reference, and localizes suspicious pixels by comparing head predictions with internal semantic evidence. It then applies feature-space and output corrections toward the inferred victim semantics, while conservative localization and output constraints limit changes to benign predictions. We model the attack as adversarial probability transport and repair as its approximate reversal, deriving bounds and sufficient conditions for localization, residual attack success, clean-side changes, defense-induced target predictions, and attack-unaware pair identification. Experiments across segmentation architectures and attack settings show that RePAIR substantially reduces attack success while largely preserving clean performance.

</details>


### [73] [Ground-to-Satellite Knowledge Distillation for Crop Classification under Temporal and Geographic Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37021)
*Miro Miranda,Rocco Sedona,Valerio Marsocci,Gabriele Cavallaro,Claudia Paris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用地面照片作为"特权监督"（photo teacher）蒸馏到卫星嵌入分类器，训练时用照片、推理时不需照片，在17类作物制图任务上稳定提升且跨时间/地理迁移更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续、更新的作物类型制图受限于参考数据稀缺；地面照片与卫星时序对同一地块提供互补视角，但推理时并非每个卫星像元都有对应地面照片，限制了其可扩展应用。

Method: 以冻结CLIP特征构建"照片教师"模型，监督基于冻结AlphaEarth与TESSERA卫星嵌入表示训练的分类器，采用知识蒸馏；照片仅在训练阶段使用，推理阶段完全依赖卫星嵌入。

Result: 在17类作物的配对地面照片-卫星影像数据集上，知识蒸馏一致优于仅用卫星的分类；对冻结嵌入，在时间偏移下增益增大，最高达+5.5 macro-F1；地理留出场景增益更大（+3.7 对比分布内 +3.0）。

Conclusion: 地面视角监督可补充卫星嵌入，提升其鲁棒性，且不改变推理阶段的数据需求，为参考数据稀缺下的可扩展作物制图提供新途径。

Abstract: Continuous and up-to-date crop type mapping is essential for agricultural monitoring but is often hampered by limited reference data. The growing availability of ground-level field photographs offers a promising way to address this challenge. When combined with satellite time series, these images provide complementary perspectives on the same parcel, capturing crop characteristics from distinctly different perspectives. However, field photographs are not always available for each satellite pixel at inference time, limiting their potential in scalable crop type mapping. We investigate whether ground photographs can provide privileged supervision for crop classification built on pretrained satellite embeddings. A photo teacher based on frozen CLIP features supervises classifiers trained on AlphaEarth and TESSERA representations; photographs are used only during training. On a paired field photo and satellite imagery dataset with 17 crop classes, knowledge distillation consistently improves over satellite-only classification; for frozen embeddings the margin grows under temporal shift, reaching up to +5.5 macro-F1 points, and widens in geographic holdouts (+3.7 vs +3.0 in-distribution). These results show that ground-view supervision can complement satellite embeddings and improve their robustness without changing inference requirements.

</details>


### [74] [Interpretable Deep Learning Models for Breast Cancer Diagnosis Using Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37026)
*Sami Alnawali,Muhammad Atif Saeed,Abdul Hadi,Azka Atiq,Akhtar Jamil,Ahmad Din*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对10种ImageNet预训练模型在BreakHis乳腺癌组织病理二分类任务上进行基准测试，并用Grad-CAM、LIME、Integrated Gradients、LRP进行可解释性分析。DenseNet121最优（99.4%准确率，AUC 0.999），Transformer类模型落后于CNN，各解释方法一致聚焦于富细胞核与腺体区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在乳腺癌诊断中表现出潜力，但可解释性不足阻碍临床落地，需系统评估模型性能与解释一致性。

Method: 在BreakHis数据集上采用患者级划分（patient-level split），对ResNet50、VGG19、InceptionV3、DenseNet121、Xception、MobileNetV2、EfficientNet、ConvNeXt、ViT、Swin Transformer十种ImageNet预训练模型进行良恶性分类基准测试，并分别用Grad-CAM、LIME、Integrated Gradients、LRP四种归因方法解释预测。

Result: DenseNet121表现最佳，测试准确率99.4%、AUC-ROC 0.999，显著优于其他所有模型（p < 0.001）；Transformer类模型性能弱于CNN；四种解释方法一致定位到具有诊断意义的富细胞核与腺体区域。

Conclusion: 迁移学习结合可解释AI可构建准确且可解释的乳腺癌诊断工具。

Abstract: Deep learning shows promise for breast cancer diagnosis, but limited interpretability hinders clinical adoption. We benchmark ten ImageNet-pretrained models (ResNet50, VGG19, InceptionV3, DenseNet121, Xception, MobileNetV2, EfficientNet, ConvNeXt, ViT, and Swin Transformer) for benign-versus-malignant classification on the BreakHis histopathology dataset using a patient-level split, and explain their predictions with Grad-CAM, LIME, Integrated Gradients, and LRP. DenseNet121 performed best, with 99.4% test accuracy and an AUC-ROC of 0.999, and significantly outperformed all other models (p < 0.001). Transformer-based models trailed the CNNs. The explanations consistently highlighted nuclei-rich and glandular regions of diagnostic relevance. Combining transfer learning with explainable AI can thus yield accurate, interpretable tools for breast cancer diagnosis.

</details>


### [75] [GraftRay: Physically Based Mesh Insertion into Gaussian Splatting Scenes via Path Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37036)
*Jinxin Ai,Matthias Niessner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GraftRay，在2D/3D高斯场景中物理一致地插入网格物体：用扩散先验恢复材质与法线，显式建模空间扩展光源并闭式求解辐亮度，混合渲染器联合路径追踪高斯与网格，实现视角一致的着色、互反射与软阴影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将虚拟物体插入由2DGS/3DGS重建的场景是内容创作、AR与合成数据生成的关键能力，但需物体被场景真实照亮并向场景投射物理合理阴影。现有方法或在图像空间做外观协调（损失多视角一致性），或在插入点用环境贴图近似场景光照，无法表征窗户、灯具等主导室内照明的近场面光源。

Method: 统一的网格物体插入框架，兼容2D与3D高斯表示：借助扩散先验恢复场景材质与表面法线；显式建模空间扩展发光体，并以闭式形式求解其辐亮度；采用混合渲染器对场景高斯与插入网格进行联合路径追踪。

Result: 在FIPT数据集上达到新SOTA，在渲染保真度与阴影准确性上优于ComGS、EAG-PT等近期基线，同时保持极具竞争力的渲染效率。

Conclusion: 显式建模近场面光源并结合高斯-网格联合路径追踪，可有效解决高斯重建场景中物体插入的光照一致性与软阴影问题，且适配2DGS与3DGS两种表示。

Abstract: Inserting virtual objects into scenes reconstructed via 2D or 3D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS/3DGS) is a key capability for content creation, AR, and synthetic data generation. However, realistic insertion requires the object to be seamlessly lit by the scene and, conversely, to cast physically plausible shadows onto it. Existing approaches either harmonize appearance in image space, sacrificing multi-view consistency, or approximate scene illumination with an environment map at the insertion point, which cannot represent near-field area lights such as windows and lamps that dominate indoor lighting. To address this, we present GraftRay, a unified method for physically consistent insertion of mesh objects into indoor scenes represented by either 2D or 3D Gaussians. We recover scene materials and surface normals with diffusion-based priors, and explicitly model spatially-extended emitters, solving their radiance in closed form. A hybrid renderer then jointly path-traces the scene Gaussians and inserted meshes, producing view-consistent shading, interreflections, and soft shadows. On FIPT, GraftRay sets a new state of the art, outperforming recent baselines such as ComGS and EAG-PT in rendering fidelity and shadow accuracy, while maintaining highly competitive rendering efficiency.

</details>


### [76] [Zooming without Zooming v2: From Seeing Details to Connecting Evidence for Generalizable Multimodal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37037)
*Lai Wei,Yatai Li,Liangbo He,Jun Lan,Renjie Gu,Linhan Cao,Junkai Lin,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Linghe Kong,Yue Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang,Weiran Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EvidenceConnect 数据合成框架，通过单图内相关区域的放大视图生成需比较、聚合或链式推理证据的问题，并用多 VLM 一致性校验与区域消融筛选高质量样本，再以 RLVR 训练得到 ZwZ2，在空间感知、长上下文理解、具身推理与智能体任务上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间感知与多模态长上下文理解是具身智能和多模态智能体的关键基础能力，但针对这两类任务的高质量训练数据合成困难且成本高昂。作者的核心洞察是：二者共享同一底层能力——"证据连接"（识别相关视觉事实、绑定到正确实体、恢复其关系并组合成答案），而针对该共享能力合成数据远比直接针对下游任务合成数据容易。

Method: 提出 EvidenceConnect 数据合成框架：利用单张图像中相关区域的放大视图（zoomed views）构造需要跨区域比较、聚合或链式推理的问题。质量控制采用两道机制：(1) 参考答案仅在多个独立 VLM 达成一致时保留；(2) 区域消融测试，若答案在移除选定区域后仍可得出，则丢弃该问题。随后使用 RLVR 在合成数据上训练基础 VLM，得到 ZwZ2。

Result: 尽管合成数据未针对任何下游基准定制，ZwZ2 仍提升了细粒度与空间感知能力，并在具身空间推理、多模态长上下文理解、多模态智能体任务及通用视觉推理上获得进一步增益。

Conclusion: EvidenceConnect 是监督"证据连接"能力的有效框架，强化这一共享能力可带来更具泛化性的多模态感知能力，验证了"以共享底层能力为训练目标"这一数据合成范式的可行性。

Abstract: Multimodal perception forms the foundation of multimodal intelligence. In particular, spatial perception and multimodal long-context understanding are two pivotal tasks that underpin embodied AI and multimodal agents. However, synthesizing targeted, high-quality training data for these tasks remains difficult and costly. Our key insight is that these tasks share an underlying capability, which we call connecting evidence: identifying relevant visual facts, binding them to the correct entities, recovering their relations, and composing them into an answer. Crucially, targeted training data are far easier to synthesize for this capability than for these tasks themselves. To strengthen this capability, we propose EvidenceConnect, an easy-to-implement data synthesis framework that uses zoomed views of related regions within a single image to generate questions requiring comparison, aggregation, or chaining of visual evidence. To ensure quality, a reference answer is retained only when independent VLMs agree on it, and region-ablation tests discard questions whose answer survives the removal of selected regions. We then train base VLMs on the resulting data with RLVR, obtaining Zooming without Zooming v2 (ZwZ2). Although the synthesized data are not tailored to any downstream benchmark, ZwZ2 improves fine-grained and spatial perception, with further gains on embodied spatial reasoning, multimodal long-context understanding, multimodal agent tasks, and general visual reasoning. These results establish EvidenceConnect as an effective framework for supervising evidence connection and suggest that strengthening this shared capability leads to more generalizable multimodal perception.

</details>


### [77] [Learning to Code the World: Harness Evolution for Compositional 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37046)
*Haoyu Yao,Chenguo Lin,Ruize Du,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SceneEvolve 框架，将 3D 场景表示为可执行代码，通过进化小型开源智能体周围的 harness 实现可编辑、可组合的 3D 场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张真实图像重建可编辑、可组合的 3D 场景需要联合推理几何、物体结构与空间关系，现有方法难以兼顾；同时希望用较小开源模型替代大型专有模型以降低成本与推理时间。

Method: SceneEvolve 由两部分组成：SceneAct——基于参数化基元、分层组合与语义空间关系的组合式语义动作接口；SceneHarness-RSI——利用更强的教师智能体，根据开发集重建反馈迭代进化 harness。整个进化过程中模型参数、动作语义与执行后端保持固定，最终得到可复用的重建流程，使小模型无需参数更新或教师协助即可重建未见场景。

Result: 在两个域内数据集与一个域外数据集上，SceneEvolve 配合较小的 Qwen3.8-27B 提升全部四项重建指标，平均推理时间相对基座模型降低 47.0%；重建质量比大型专有模型 GPT-5.6-Sol 高 32.3%，平均等效部署成本降低 59.8%；且该 harness 可迁移至 Kimi-K3，达到与 GPT-6-Astra 相当的重建性能。

Conclusion: 进化得到的 harness 可复用、可跨模型迁移，使小型开源智能体以更低成本和时间获得优于大型专有模型的重建质量，验证了以代码表示场景并结合 harness 进化的有效性。

Abstract: Reconstructing editable and compositional 3D scenes from a real image requires jointly reasoning about geometry, object structure, and spatial relationships. We introduce SceneEvolve, a framework that represents 3D scenes as executable code and improves reconstruction by evolving the harness around a small open-source agent. SceneEvolve combines SceneAct, a compositional semantic action interface built on parameterized primitives, hierarchical composition, and semantic spatial relations, with SceneHarness-RSI, which leverages a stronger teacher agent to iteratively evolve the harness using feedback from development-set reconstructions. Throughout harness evolution, the model parameters, action semantics, and execution backend remain fixed. The resulting harness provides a reusable reconstruction procedure that enables the small open-source model to reconstruct unseen scenes without parameter updates or teacher assistance. Across two in-domain datasets and one out-of-domain dataset, SceneEvolve with the comparatively compact Qwen3.8-27B model improves all four evaluated reconstruction metrics while reducing mean inference time by 47.0% relative to the base model. More notably, despite relying on a substantially smaller model, SceneEvolve achieves 32.3% higher reconstruction quality than the large proprietary GPT-5.6-Sol model, while reducing mean equivalent deployment cost by 59.8%. Furthermore, the harness evolved using Qwen3.8-27B also generalizes to Kimi-K3, achieving comparable reconstruction performance to GPT-6-Astra. The project page is https://haoyu-agent.github.io/SceneEvolve.

</details>


### [78] [Systematic Morphometric Brain Autoencoding for Representation Learning in Neurodegenerative Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37074)
*Nuria Balbás-Esteban,Albert Belenguer-Llorens,Elaheh Moradi,Jussi Tohka,Vanessa Gómez-Verdejo,Carlos Sevilla-Salcedo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SYMBA自监督预训练框架，面向结构T1脑MRI，以解剖约束掩码、MS-SSIM重建与渐进课程显式注入神经解剖先验；在9个异质队列6953例扫描上预训练，于两个未见数据集冻结线性探测下优于现有神经影像基础模型（AD分类AUROC 0.930，MCI转化0.772），且仅用6例标注即达约0.90 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结构T1 MRI的神经退行性疾病早期识别需泛化可靠的模型，但监督深度学习受标注稀缺与扫描仪导致的分布偏移制约；现有自监督方法多沿袭自然图像范式（对比学习或体素级通用掩码重建），难以刻画神经退行性变所对应的分布式形态学模式。

Method: SYMBA（SYstematic Morphometric Brain Autoencoding）自监督预训练框架，三个核心组件：①解剖约束掩码；②多尺度结构相似性（MS-SSIM）重建目标；③由局部解剖细节过渡到全局脑形态的渐进课程。整体将神经解剖结构显式纳入自监督目标，而非依赖通用图像先验。预训练数据为9个异质队列共6953例T1加权MRI；评估采用冻结线性探测，在两个独立的未见数据集上检验分布外泛化。

Result: 在多任务上一致优于近期神经影像基础模型：AD分类AUROC 0.930，MCI转化预测0.772，淀粉样蛋白状态预测表现良好；低标注场景下样本效率高，仅用6例标注受试者即达约0.90 AUROC（AD/CN分类）。

Conclusion: 面向结构脑MRI的自监督目标中融入解剖先验可显著提升分布外泛化与标注效率，为神经退行性疾病早期筛查提供了可迁移的预训练范式。

Abstract: Early identification of neurodegenerative disorders using structural T1-weighted MRI is essential for timely clinical intervention, yet supervised deep learning models often struggle to generalize due to limited annotations and scanner-induced distribution shift. Self-supervised learning offers a promising alternative; however, existing approaches are largely adapted from natural image paradigms, relying on contrastive objectives or generic masked reconstruction with voxel-wise losses, which may not fully capture the distributed morphometric patterns associated with neurodegeneration.
  We introduce SYMBA (SYstematic Morphometric Brain Autoencoding), a self-supervised pretraining framework specifically designed for structural brain MRI. SYMBA integrates anatomically constrained masking, a multi-scale structural similarity (MS-SSIM) reconstruction objective, and a progressive curriculum that transitions from local anatomical detail to global brain morphology. These components explicitly incorporate neuroanatomical structure into the self-supervised objective rather than relying on generic image priors.
  Pretrained on 6,953 T1-weighted MRI scans from nine heterogeneous cohorts, SYMBA is evaluated using frozen linear probing on two independent unseen datasets, enabling a strict assessment of out-of-distribution generalization. Across multiple clinical tasks, SYMBA consistently outperforms recent neuroimaging foundation models, achieving 0.930 AUROC for Alzheimer's disease classification, 0.772 for mild cognitive impairment conversion prediction, and strong performance in amyloid status prediction. In low-label regimes, SYMBA demonstrates high sample efficiency, reaching approximately 0.90 AUROC for AD/CN classification using only six labeled subjects.

</details>


### [79] [Learning to Look Beyond Screens: Transferable Visual Observation from GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37093)
*Ruilin Yao,Xinyu Qiu,Tianyu Zou,Yi Rong,Shengwu Xiong,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 LBS（Look Beyond Screens），将 GUI grounding 作为可扩展的空间监督来源，通过“SFT 初始化 + 多轮裁剪-检视强化学习”训练视觉语言模型，使主动观察行为可迁移至 GUI 之外的感知与多模态推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉感知正从被动识别转向主动、任务导向的观察，但缺乏可扩展的空间监督信号。GUI grounding 天然要求精细定位与局部查看，作者探究其能否作为训练通用主动观察行为的监督范式。

Method: 两阶段训练：①在教师模型生成的 GUI grounding 轨迹上做监督微调以完成初始化，习得视觉检视原语；②用强化学习优化多轮 crop-and-inspect（裁剪—检视）决策。基于 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 与 Qwen3.8-27B 两个骨干构建模型，并在固定推理设置下与对应基线对比。

Result: 固定推理配置下，两个骨干上的 LBS 均稳定优于对应基线；超越 SFT 的增益可迁移到 PerceptionBench（两个骨干）、HR-Bench 与 ChartQAPro（Qwen3.5-35B-A3B），表明观察策略不局限于 GUI 特定模仿。消融分析显示两阶段互补：SFT 建立视觉检视原语，RL 提升其下游有效性。

Conclusion: GUI grounding 可作为学习可迁移主动视觉感知的实用监督范式，并有望服务于更广泛的多模态推理；代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Visual perception is evolving from passive recognition toward active, task-oriented observation. We investigate GUI grounding as a scalable source of spatial supervision for learning observation behaviors that transfer beyond interfaces. We introduce LBS (Look Beyond Screens), a vision-language model trained through supervised initialization on teacher-generated GUI grounding trajectories, followed by reinforcement learning for multi-turn crop-and-inspect decisions. Under fixed inference settings, LBS models based on Qwen3.5-35B-A3B and Qwen3.8-27B consistently improve over their corresponding baselines. Gains beyond supervised fine-tuning transfer to PerceptionBench for both backbones and to HR-Bench and ChartQAPro for Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, supporting observation strategies that generalize beyond GUI-specific imitation. Further analysis suggests complementary roles for the two stages: SFT establishes visual inspection primitives, while RL improves their downstream effectiveness. These findings support GUI grounding as a practical supervision paradigm for transferable active visual perception and broader multimodal reasoning. Our code and model are publicly available at https://github.com/inclusionAI/UI-Venus/tree/LBS and https://huggingface.co/inclusionAI/LBS-27B, respectively.

</details>


### [80] [TriDifNet: A Unified Three-Task Diffusion Network for End-to-End RGB-T Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37097)
*Qianchao He,Jing Liu,Yunhao Li,Xiaoqing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的扩散式三任务网络 TriDifNet，联合优化配准、融合与检测，在 KAIST 错位 RGB-T 场景下取得更好的下游行人检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外-可见光多模态感知在恶劣光照下提供稳健场景理解，但平台运动、视角变化及模态相关畸变导致红外与可见图像常处于错位状态，配准误差会传播至后续融合与检测阶段。现有流程多为串联式，缺乏三任务间的联合优化。

Method: TriDifNet 由三个基于扩散的模块组成：配准扩散模块 RegDM 通过估计稠密变形场将未对齐红外图像对齐至可见光参考；融合扩散模块 FusDM 采用残差条件扩散，生成兼具可见结构线索与红外显著信息、面向检测的融合表示；检测扩散模块 DetDM 将行人检测建模为边界框空间的迭代去噪过程。为耦合三任务，引入闭环优化机制：检测语义指导融合与配准，融合结果进一步正则化几何对齐。训练上采用分阶段渐进策略，并在后期引入基于 Nash-MTL 的轻量多任务精调以平衡三任务优化。

Result: 在 KAIST 多光谱行人基准上的大量实验表明，TriDifNet 在错位 RGB-T 设置下取得优于强系统级对比方法的下游检测性能：Recall 65.44%、mAP50 57.55%、mAP50:95 26.83%。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，TriDifNet 是首个针对错位 RGB-T 行人感知、以扩散网络端到端联合优化配准、融合与检测的框架，验证了三任务闭环耦合与渐进训练策略的有效性。

Abstract: Infrared--visible (IR--VIS) multi-modal perception supports robust scene understanding under challenging illumination. Infrared and visible images are often misaligned due to platform motion, viewpoint changes, and modality-dependent distortions, causing registration errors to propagate into subsequent fusion and detection stages. We propose TriDifNet, a unified three-task diffusion network for end-to-end RGB-T perception. To the best of our knowledge, TriDifNet is the first diffusion-based network that jointly optimizes registration, fusion, and detection for misaligned RGB-T pedestrian perception. TriDifNet consists of three diffusion-based modules, i.e., the Registration Diffusion Module (RegDM), the Fusion Diffusion Module (FusDM), and the Detection Diffusion Module (DetDM). RegDM aligns the unaligned infrared image to the optical reference by estimating a dense deformation field. FusDM performs residual conditional diffusion to generate a detection-oriented fused representation with optical structural cues and complementary infrared saliency. DetDM formulates pedestrian detection as an iterative denoising process in bounding-box space. To couple the three tasks, TriDifNet introduces a closed-loop optimization mechanism, where detection semantics guide fusion and registration, and the fusion result further regularizes geometric alignment. A stage-wise progressive training strategy and a lightweight Nash multi-task learning (Nash-MTL)-based late-stage refinement are further adopted to balance registration, fusion, and detection optimization. Extensive experiments on the KAIST multispectral pedestrian benchmark show that TriDifNet achieves superior downstream detection performance under misaligned RGB-T settings. Against strong system-level competitors, TriDifNet achieves 65.44\% Recall, 57.55\% mAP$_{50}$, and 26.83\% mAP$_{50:95}$.

</details>


### [81] [SplatScout: Query-Driven Part-Level Referring Segmentation in Gaussian Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37098)
*Yihan Zang,Da Li,Shinkyu Park,Ivan Viola,Dominik Engel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SplatScout，一种由VLM主动控制的查询驱动闭环高斯场景指代分割方法，可在部件级别实现精细分割，并构建PartRef-ScanNet++基准，取得0.538 mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯场景指代分割主要针对整体对象，依赖分割器预生成候选再由视觉语言模型（VLM）挑选，这类固定候选可能遗漏作为目标或识别线索所需的细粒度部件。

Method: SplatScout将感知置于VLM控制之下形成闭环：模型主动选择视角、指导分割，并检视渲染的三维结果以迭代修正预测；同时构建PartRef-ScanNet++基准，含12个ScanNet++场景中261条人工核验查询及网格导出的真值。

Result: 在PartRef-ScanNet++上达到0.538 mIoU，显著优于现有方法；消融实验验证迭代修正的收益，不同VLM骨干对比凸显有效工具反馈利用的重要性；在四个现有指代基准上也取得更高分数。

Conclusion: 将感知置于VLM的主动闭环控制下可有效实现部件级指代分割，迭代修正与工具反馈利用是性能提升的关键；代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: Existing referring segmentation methods on Gaussian scenes primarily target whole objects, relying on segmenter-generated candidates from which a vision-language model (VLM) selects. Such predetermined fixed candidates may omit fine-grained parts needed as targets or cues for identifying objects. We introduce SplatScout, a query-driven, closed-loop approach that places perception under VLM control. The model actively selects views, directs segmentation, and inspects rendered 3D results to refine its predictions. For part-level evaluation, we introduce PartRef-ScanNet++, comprising 261 manually verified queries across 12 ScanNet++ scenes with mesh-derived ground truth. On PartRef-ScanNet++, SplatScout achieves 0.538 mIoU, substantially outperforming existing methods. Ablations demonstrate the benefits of iterative refinement, while comparisons across VLM backbones highlight the importance of effective tool-feedback utilization. SplatScout also achieves higher scores on four existing referring benchmarks. Code and the PartRef-ScanNet++ benchmark are available at https://github.com/kaust-violab/SplatScout.

</details>


### [82] [A First-Step Bias Restores Lost Diversity in Diffusion Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37110)
*Wenwu Tang,Dong Wang,Yun Cheng,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReSeed：仅对蒸馏学生模型的第一步加一个种子响应偏置适配器，并与教师首个区间的割线速度对齐，以极轻量、数据高效的方式恢复少步生成模型的输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布匹配蒸馏可实现少步高质量生成，但蒸馏后学生在同一提示下会把不同初始噪声映射为相似输出，多样性显著下降；作者希望定位多样性究竟在哪一步丢失，以便有针对性地恢复。

Method: 沿采样轨迹对比教师与学生：发现对不同初始噪声的响应衰减最大的是第一个高噪声步；将首步替换为教师轨迹、其余三步保持不变即可恢复大部分多样性。据此提出后蒸馏方法ReSeed，仅在学生第一步加入seed-response bias adapter，并将其速度与教师第一区间的割线速度（secant velocity）对齐；学生全部权重与后续去噪步冻结不变，无需目标图像、感知损失或对抗损失。

Result: 在SD3-Medium、SD3.5-Medium、FLUX.1-dev三个文生图模型和Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B文生视频模型上评估。4步蒸馏FLUX.1-dev上，ReSeed仅增加相对基模型0.00196%的可训练参数，用8000个提示–噪声对训练，DINO多样性提升48.3%，同时保持有竞争力的视觉质量。

Conclusion: 多样性损失主要集中于首步高噪声阶段；仅对第一步做轻量、数据高效的调优即可恢复大部分多样性，无需微调整个学生模型，为少步蒸馏模型的多样性修复提供了低成本方案。

Abstract: Distribution-matching distillation enables high-quality image generation in only a few denoising steps. However, distilled student models often map different initial noise samples to similar outputs under the same prompt. To locate where diversity is lost, we compare the teacher and student along the sampling trajectory. The largest measured reduction in the response to different initial noises occurs during the first, high-noise step. When we replace the first step with the teacher's corresponding trajectory and leave the final three student steps unchanged, most of the output diversity returns. This suggests that the first step is a key point for restoring diversity. Motivated by this finding, we introduce \emph{ReSeed}, a post-distillation method that adds a seed-response bias adapter only to the first student step and aligns its velocity with the teacher's first-interval secant velocity. All student weights and later denoising steps remain unchanged. We evaluate ReSeed on three text-to-image models (SD3-Medium, SD3.5-Medium, and FLUX.1-dev) and one text-to-video model (Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B). For a distilled 4-step FLUX.1-dev model, \emph{ReSeed} requires only $0.00196\%$ additional trainable parameters relative to the base model and is trained on 8,000 prompt--noise pairs, while keeping the distilled student fully frozen and requiring no target images, perceptual losses, or adversarial losses. It improves DINO diversity by $48.3\%$ while maintaining competitive visual quality. These results show that substantial diversity can be recovered through a lightweight, data-efficient tuning of only the first denoising step, without finetuning the full student model.

</details>


### [83] [Multibranch Ensemble Head in Vision Foundation Models for Efficient Cucumber Phenological Stage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37118)
*Kostiantyn Khabarlak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多分支集成头（multi-branch ensemble head）：训练时随机采样单一分支、推理时在概率空间平均低/高容量两分支，以极低参数与推理开销提升小样本域偏移下的视觉基础模型适配性能，并在黄瓜物候期分类任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督预训练的视觉基础模型（如DINOv2）特征迁移性强，但在小规模、存在域偏移的数据集上微调仍具挑战；同时现有高效适配方案常在精度与效率间难以兼顾。本文以黄瓜物候期分类（花与果常被叶片遮挡、阶段转变主要表现为叶片形态的细微变化）作为具有挑战性的实际场景。

Method: 设计正则化多分支集成头：将低容量与高容量分支配对，每个训练步随机采样单个分支进行训练（起到正则化/子网络采样作用），推理时对两个分支的输出在概率空间取平均。该结构仅增加可忽略的参数，相对单个重型头几乎无额外推理开销。在NALEF食品安全数据集的五类黄瓜生长阶段图像上，系统对比DINOv2、DINOv3的ViT与ConvNeXt变体，以及ResNet、EfficientNet、ViT等强监督模型。

Result: DINOv2 ViT-S 搭配多分支头取得 Macro F1 = 0.978、Cohen's Kappa = 0.971，性能与参数和GFlops超过其十倍以上的模型相当，而自身仅需12.2 GFlops、22.3M参数，可部署于智能手机与边缘设备。

Conclusion: 以极小的参数和推理代价即可显著改善数据稀缺条件下基础模型适配的精度-效率折中，多分支集成头是一种轻量、实用的正则化适配策略，适用于边缘端物候期分类等实际应用。

Abstract: Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) pretrained with self-supervised learning, such as DINOv2, provide highly transferable features, yet adapting them to small, domain-shifted datasets is still challenging. We propose a regularizing multibranch ensemble head: a training and inference scheme that pairs low- and high-capacity branches, samples a single branch stochastically at each training step, and averages both branches in probability space at inference. The design adds negligible parameters and essentially no inference overhead over a single heavy head, while improving the accuracy-efficiency trade-off under data-scarce conditions. Accurate classification of plant growth stages is critical for precision agriculture, enabling timely decisions on fertilization, irrigation, and harvesting. Therefore, as a challenging practical case we use cucumber phenological stage classification, in which flowers and fruits are frequently occluded by foliage, and stage transitions manifest primarily as subtle changes in leaf morphology. The NALEF Food Safety dataset contains images of five cucumber growth stages captured under diverse real-world conditions. On it we evaluate a broad range of backbones, including DINOv2 and DINOv3 ViT and ConvNeXt variants, against strongly supervised counterparts (ResNet, EfficientNet, ViT). The proposed DINOv2 ViT-S with multibranch head achieves a Macro F1 of 0.978 and Cohen's Kappa of 0.971, matching the performance of models more than ten times larger in parameters and GFlops, while requiring only 12.2 GFlops and 22.3M parameters, making it deployable on smartphones and edge devices for phenological stage classification.

</details>


### [84] [MCP-Net: Real-Time Coronary Perception Network and Topological Analysis for Robotic Guidewire Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37127)
*Sahar Nasirihaghighi,Tianyuan Wang,Kenan Niu,Stefano Stramigioli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仿真驱动的多任务冠脉感知框架MCP-Net，从单帧透视图像同时完成血管分割、导丝尖端定位与狭窄定位，并转换为结构化血管图，自动生成通往病灶的路径图，支撑机器人导丝导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助PCI中，导丝导航需在二维X射线透视下连续判读血管解剖、导丝位置与目标狭窄。现有方法多将血管分割与器械定位作为独立任务处理，缺乏面向下游导航的结构化场景感知。

Method: 基于三维冠脉CTA生成仿真透视图像（含血管结构、仿真导丝、不同严重程度狭窄及多样血管几何形态）及仿真真值；MCP-Net以单帧透视图像为输入，联合执行冠脉分割、导丝尖端定位与狭窄定位；随后将预测结果转换为含中心线、分叉点、端点、分支方向与分支角的结构化血管图，并将预测狭窄与图关联，从自动选取的图根节点生成至目标病灶的路径图。

Result: 结果表明该框架可联合恢复多项冠脉感知目标，并将其转化为可解释、适用于下游机器人导航的血管表示。

Conclusion: 以多任务感知加结构化血管图表示的统一框架，可替代割裂的单任务方案，为机器人导丝导航提供结构化、可解释的冠脉场景感知。

Abstract: Coronary guidewire navigation during percutaneous coronary intervention requires continuous interpretation of vessel anatomy, guidewire position, and target stenosis under two-dimensional X-ray fluoroscopy. While existing approaches commonly address vessel segmentation or device localization as separate tasks, a robotic system requires a more structured perception of the coronary scene to support downstream navigation. In this work, we present a simulation-driven framework centered on a Multi-Task Coronary Perception Network (MCP-Net) for robotic guidewire navigation, integrating coronary vessel segmentation, vascular topology extraction, and roadmap generation. Starting from three-dimensional coronary CT angiography, we generate simulated fluoroscopy images containing vascular structures, simulated guidewires, stenotic lesions with varying severity, and diverse vascular geometries, together with simulation-derived ground truth. MCP-Net jointly performs coronary vessel segmentation, guidewire-tip localization, and stenosis localization from a single fluoroscopic frame. These predictions are subsequently converted into a structured vascular graph containing centerlines, bifurcations, endpoints, branch directions, and branch angles. By associating the predicted stenosis with this graph, the framework generates a roadmap from the automatically selected graph root toward the target lesion. The results demonstrate that the proposed framework can jointly recover coronary perception targets and transform them into an interpretable vascular representation suitable for downstream robotic navigation.

</details>


### [85] [MRI-to-CT Translation of Spinal Images via Anatomy-Guided Pseudo-Pairing and Adversarial Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37134)
*Eduardo Nieto,Ibai Genua,Davide Scorza,Karen López-Linares*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段脊柱MRI→CT合成框架：解剖引导的椎体匹配生成伪配对数据、对抗扩散模型做图像翻译、3D体数据后处理提升层间一致性；二值3D椎体Dice从0.666提升至0.739。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI是脊柱疾病常规诊断手段，但手术规划常需CT以更好显示椎体骨性解剖；由MRI合成CT可避免额外辐射暴露并简化术前评估流程，而临床数据中配对的MRI-CT稀缺。

Method: 三阶段框架：①解剖引导的椎体匹配系统，基于脊柱分割识别相似矢状位MRI/CT切片并经刚性配准对齐，从非配对扫描构建伪配对数据；②用这些配对训练对抗扩散模型实现MRI→CT翻译；③将合成切片重组为3D体数据，由网络精修以改善层间连续性与结构一致性。

Result: 在超过10,000张非配对矢状位MRI与CT切片上训练，在覆盖全脊柱区域的83个MRI体数据共1,911张切片上评估；结合分布度量与椎体分割指标。相比纯扩散方法，二值3D椎体Dice由0.666提升至0.739；体数据后处理减少了堆叠切片间的可见不连续，并改善了轴位与冠状位的椎体一致性。

Conclusion: 解剖引导伪配对、对抗扩散与3D精修相结合可提升脊柱MRI→CT合成质量：椎体匹配主要增强解剖对齐与保真度，体数据精修改善3D一致性；支持进一步发展用于术前规划的MRI衍生类CT可视化，尤其需在保持体数据一致性的同时保留细小强度细节。

Abstract: Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is routinely used to diagnose spinal disorders, but surgical planning often requires Computed Tomography (CT) for better visualization of vertebral bone anatomy. Synthesizing CT from MRI could avoid additional radiation exposure and streamline preoperative assessment.
  We propose a three-stage framework for spine MRI-to-CT synthesis. First, an anatomy-guided vertebral matching system identifies similar sagittal MRI and CT slices using spine segmentations and aligns them through rigid registration, creating pseudo-paired data from unpaired scans. Second, these pairs train an adversarial diffusion model for MRI-to-CT translation. Third, synthesized slices are assembled into 3D volumes and refined by a network that improves inter-slice continuity and structural coherence.
  The framework was trained on over 10,000 unpaired sagittal MRI and CT slices and evaluated on 1,911 slices from 83 MRI volumes spanning all spinal regions. Assessment combined distribution-based measures with vertebral segmentation metrics. Compared with diffusion alone, the proposed approach increased binary 3D vertebral Dice from 0.666 to 0.739. Volumetric post-processing reduced visible discontinuities between stacked slices and improved vertebral coherence in axial and coronal views.
  Combining anatomy-guided pseudo-pairing, adversarial diffusion and 3D refinement improves spine MRI-to-CT synthesis. Vertebral matching primarily enhanced anatomical alignment and fidelity, while volumetric refinement improved 3D consistency. These findings support further development of MRI-derived CT-like visualization for preoperative planning, particularly to preserve fine intensity details while maintaining volumetric coherence.

</details>


### [86] [Physics Guided Diffusion Model Based Exposure Interpolation for High Dynamic Range Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37135)
*Zhengguo Li,Chaobing Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种物理引导的扩散模型曝光插值算法，用于单次拍摄双低曝光比SDR图像的高动态范围成像，消除亮度顺序反转（BOR）伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多帧HDR存在鬼影问题；单次拍摄两组大曝光比（LER）SDR图像可避免鬼影，但直接融合会产生亮度顺序反转（BOR）伪影，因此需要在两个LER曝光时间之间插值出一幅中间曝光图像。

Method: 物理驱动组件先插值出初始图像，再由新设计的扩散模型组件生成残余高频细节；该扩散模型基于生成模型的相关性分析，引入新的预测目标，以降低噪声数据与目标之间Pearson相关性的波动，并抑制拟合误差的放大因子。

Result: 实验验证了所提曝光插值算法的有效性；相较现有基于卷积神经网络的神经增强曝光插值算法，其在生成高频信息方面具有内在优势。

Conclusion: 物理引导+扩散模型的曝光插值可有效缓解BOR伪影，并为单次拍摄HDR提供更丰富高频细节的解决方案。

Abstract: Ghosting artifacts are an open problem for popular multi-shot based high dynamic range (HDR) imaging. An effective solution is to capture two large-exposure-ratio (LER) standard dynamic range (SDR) images simultaneously via a single-shot. Brightness order reversal (BOR) artifacts are generated when the two LER images are directly fused together. It is crucial to interpolate an image with an exposure time in between the exposure times of two LER images. In this paper, a novel physics-guided diffusion model based exposure interpolation algorithm is proposed to address the BOR artifacts. An initial image is interpolated by a physics-driven component, and the residual high-frequency details are generated by a new diffusion model based component. The new diffusion model is based on the correlation analysis of generative models and includes a new predicted target to reduce the variation of the Pearson correlation between the noisy data and the target, as well as the amplification factor of the fitting error. Compared with existing neural augmented exposure interpolation algorithms on top of convolution neural networks, the proposed exposure interpolation algorithm has an inherent advantage to generate more high frequency information. Experiment results validate the proposed exposure interpolation algorithm.

</details>


### [87] [MixSIS: Mixed-supervised instance segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37138)
*Christian Wilms,Emre Gülsoylu,Sven Schreiber,Simone Frintrop*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混合监督实例分割任务MixSIS，允许数据集内灵活混用image/point/box/mask标注，并给出基线方法AnnsMixer，可从各标注级别（甚至单图内混合）生成伪mask，性能大幅超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督实例分割（WSIS）方法多局限于单一标注级别，忽略了不同目标标注难度不同：简单目标点标注即可，困难目标需box甚至mask。故需支持标注级别灵活混合的设定以优化标注成本。

Method: 定义MixSIS任务；提出AnnsMixer基线，作为真正混合监督的实例分割系统，从所有标注级别（含单张图像内混合的标注）生成伪mask标注用于训练。

Result: 在WSIS与MixSIS两个任务上均大幅超越现有SOTA；三个案例研究表明，借助简单标注规则或异构数据，MixSIS与AnnsMixer可将标注时间减少约90%或将结果提升约34%。

Conclusion: 混合监督为实例分割提供了实用且高效的标注范式，AnnsMixer验证了跨标注级别生成伪mask的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Weakly-supervised instance segmentation (WSIS) based on either box-, point-, or image-level annotations has been proposed to overcome the labor-intensive annotation process in fully-supervised instance segmentation. Despite decent results, most WSIS methods only focus on a single annotation level, ignoring that some objects are easy to spot from point annotations, while others require box or even mask annotations. Therefore, we propose the Mixed-Supervised Instance Segmentation (MixSIS) task, which allows a flexible mix of annotation levels (image, point, box, and mask) within a dataset. MixSIS has a high practical relevance, as it allows optimizing the annotation process by annotating challenging objects in more detail, while saving time on simpler instances with less detailed annotations. To address MixSIS, we propose AnnotationsMixer (AnnsMixer) as a baseline. AnnsMixer is a truly mixed-supervised instance segmentation system, which generates pseudo mask annotations from all annotation levels, even mixed within a single image. We evaluate AnnsMixer on both WSIS and MixSIS tasks, largely outperforming state-of-the-art methods. In three case studies, we also demonstrate how the unique features of MixSIS and AnnsMixer can substantially reduce the annotation time (-90%) or improve the results (+34%) in instance segmentation with a simple annotation rule or leveraging heterogeneous data. Our code is available at https://github.com/chwilms/MixSIS

</details>


### [88] [GCSR-OCC: Geometry Calibration and Semantic Routing for Open-Vocabulary Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37149)
*Bohua Wang,Congyang Zhao,Tianxiong Lv,Chenxu Zhao,He Zheng,Hangqi Fan,Leigang Luo,Zhihao Liao,Yihong Guo,Yang Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GCSR-OCC，用几何校准（EC-G2O）与多模式查询条件语义路由（MM-QGSR）改进3D高斯到体素占用的开放词汇占用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D高斯重建并转体素占用的开放词汇占用预测存在两类问题：高斯尺度膨胀与过度重叠会扭曲体素占用预测；跨多类别的高斯出现语义混淆，且3D–2D语义特征对齐过程中也会引入混淆。

Method: 1) EC-G2O（Evidence-Conserving Gaussian-to-Occupancy）算子：采用核质量归一化、p-范数聚合与可学习增益，抑制尺度膨胀与局部证据过度累积，并校准占用响应。2) MM-QGSR（Multi-mode Query-First Gaussian Semantic Routing）：为每个高斯匹配多个语义模态到文本查询，并经由可学习的文本条件路由器将查询特定证据聚合到体素；辅以细粒度子类查询与平滑语义聚合细化体素级预测。仅需单张RGB图像。

Result: 在无3D语义标注条件下，于Occ-ScanNet上取得开放词汇占用预测SOTA：几何IoU 63.31%，语义mIoU 27.44%；几何聚合延迟从19.76 ms降至12.88 ms。

Conclusion: 通过几何证据守恒校准与查询优先的多模式语义路由，可有效缓解高斯尺度膨胀、重叠累积与语义混淆，在无3D语义监督下实现高效的开放词汇室内占用预测；代码将公开。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D occupancy prediction represents scene geometry and semantics in a unified voxel grid, enabling recognition beyond predefined categories. One approach reconstructs scenes with 3D Gaussians and converts them into voxel occupancy. However, Gaussian scale inflation and excessive overlap can distort voxel occupancy predictions, while semantic mixing can arise within Gaussians spanning multiple categories and during 3D--2D semantic feature alignment. We propose GCSR-OCC, which combines geometry calibration and multi-mode query-conditioned semantic routing for indoor occupancy prediction from a single RGB image. The Evidence-Conserving Gaussian-to-Occupancy operator (EC-G2O) uses kernel mass normalization, $p$-norm aggregation, and a learnable gain to suppress scale inflation and excessive local evidence accumulation while calibrating occupancy responses. Multi-mode Query-First Gaussian Semantic Routing (MM-QGSR) matches multiple semantic modes per Gaussian to text queries and aggregates query-specific evidence into voxels through a learnable text-conditioned router. Fine-grained subclass queries and smoothed semantic aggregation further refine voxel-level predictions. Without 3D semantic annotations, GCSR-OCC achieves state-of-the-art performance in open-vocabulary occupancy prediction on Occ-ScanNet, with 63.31% geometric IoU and 27.44% semantic mIoU. It also reduces geometric aggregation latency from 19.76 ms to 12.88 ms. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [89] [FastBEVFusion: Simple Multi-Modal Fusion for 3D Object Detection at 50 FPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37153)
*Tom Sanitz,Emre Gülsoylu,Simone Frintrop,Christian Wilms*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastBEVFusion：面向自动驾驶的快速鲁棒 LiDAR-相机融合 3D 检测器，通过避免深度估计的“半对齐”BEV 空间投影与轻量跨注意力动态融合，在 nuScenes 上实现超 50 FPS 且优于现有实时方法，并对标定误差具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠实时的 LiDAR-相机 3D 检测对自动驾驶安全至关重要，但许多 SOTA 方法虽检测质量高，却无法满足实时约束；同时基于深度估计的视图变换计算开销大且易引入误差。

Method: 提出 FastBEVFusion：将各模态特征投影到半对齐的模态专属 BEV 空间，摒弃计算密集且易出错的深度估计；并设计基于交叉注意力的轻量、软性、动态融合模块，可随场景上下文自适应，在整个 BEV 空间上完成特征融合。

Result: 在复杂 nuScenes 数据集上，FastBEVFusion 优于现有实时系统，在无部署库条件下达到 50 FPS 以上；同时系统对标定误差表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FastBEVFusion 以简单、快速、鲁棒的架构兼顾实时性与检测质量，具备实际落地价值，是面向真实场景的有力 3D 检测系统。

Abstract: Reliable real-time 3D object detection based on LiDAR and camera data is critical for safety in autonomous driving, where systems must accurately perceive their surroundings with minimal latency. Many state-of-the-art systems achieve high-quality detection results but fail to meet real-time constraints, limiting their practical use. To address this challenge, we propose FastBEVFusion, a simple, fast, and robust fusion system for 3D object detection. Based on modality-specific features, FastBEVFusion projects these features into semi-aligned modality-specific BEV spaces, without relying on a computationally intensive and potentially error-prone depth estimation. Given these BEV spaces, we propose a light-weight, soft, and dynamic fusion module based on cross-attention, which adapts dynamically to the scene context and enables feature fusion across the entire BEV spaces. Experiments on the complex nuScenes dataset show that FastBEVFusion outperforms state-of-the-art real-time systems, and achieves over 50 FPS without deployment libraries. Additionally, our system shows strong robustness to calibration errors, making FastBEVFusion a powerful 3D object detection system for real-world applications.

</details>


### [90] [INTR++: INterpretable TRansformer with better Accuracy, Interpretability, and Adaptability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37178)
*Dipanjyoti Paul,Arpita Chowdhury,Maneet Chatterjee,Daichi Kitaguchi,Tanya Berger-Wolf,Tetsuya Sakurai,Wei-Lun Chao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 INTR++，在 Prompt-CAM 基础上加入轻量级 Prompt Adapter Module (PAM)，使可学习提示能够自适应地融入域特定知识，从而在跨域场景下保持稳定的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释 Transformer 在特定域表现良好，但跨域泛化差；Prompt-CAM 虽通过可学习提示提升域适应性，但其提示是固定的，难以适应输入图像特征差异较大的域（如医学影像、显微成像、遥感影像），导致可解释性下降。

Method: INTR++ 扩展 Prompt-CAM，引入 Prompt Adapter Module (PAM)，用域特定知识丰富并自适应调制提示；保持可解释 Transformer 的标准架构与端到端训练方式，仅增加少量参数。

Result: 在动物、植物、医学影像、显微成像、遥感影像及以物体为中心的数据等多个域上，INTR++ 在各方面均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 通过轻量的提示自适应机制，可在不显著增加参数与改动架构的前提下，实现跨多样域的稳定可解释性。

Abstract: Interpretable Transformers often perform well in domain specific settings but lose effectiveness beyond that. Achieving ViT-like broad applicability requires models with consistent performance across domains. Recent works on Interpretable Transformers, INTR and Prompt-CAM, were developed keeping domain adaptability in mind. Prompt-CAM leverages learnable prompts to promote adaptability. However, these prompts are fixed in nature and therefore struggle to adapt to domains with substantial variation in input image characteristics, resulting in reduced interpretability. To address this limitation, we propose INTR++, which extends Prompt-CAM by incorporating a lightweight Prompt Adapter Module (PAM). PAM enables prompts to be adaptive by enriching them with domain-specific knowledge, thereby ensuring consistent interpretability across diverse domains including those with varying image characteristics such as medical imaging, micro-scopic imaging, and remote sensing imagery. INTR++ retains the standard architecture and end-to-end learning of Interpretable Transformer based models, while adding only a small number of additional parameters compared to Prompt-CAM. We demonstrate that INTR++ delivers consistent improvements in all aspects across domains like animals, plants, medical imaging, microscopic imaging, remote sensing imagery and object-centric data.

</details>


### [91] [VGIQA: Fine-Grained Image Quality Assessment with Visual Grounding Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37186)
*Weichang Wang,Liyu Chen,Shuyue Guan,Jie Hu,Fei Gao,Wei Li,Nannan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGIQA：具备局部+全局质量评估能力的MLLM式IQA模型，采用冷启动+强化学习两阶段训练，并构建VGSR-2K局部质量数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的IQA方法局限于整体、图像级评价，缺乏细粒度、局部化评估能力，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出VGIQA，采用冷启动（cold-start）后接强化学习的两阶段训练范式；设计局部质量评估奖励函数驱动RL训练；并构建专门用于局部质量评价的数据集VGSR-2K。

Result: 在VGSR-2K上展现出优异的图像质量理解能力，显著优于Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct、Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct等开源MLLM；局部质量评估任务还能有效提升模型在退化感知任务上的表现。

Conclusion: VGIQA同时具备局部与全局质量评估能力，验证了局部质量评估对IQA及退化感知任务的正向迁移价值。

Abstract: Image quality assessment (IQA) aims to evaluate the perceptual visual quality of images. Recently, the rapid development of Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has significantly advanced the IQA field. By integrating chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and reinforcement learning (RL), these models can generate more accurate and interpretable results while relying on less training data. However, existing MLLM-based methods are predominantly constrained to holistic, image-level evaluations and lack the capability to produce fine-grained, localized assessments, which substantially limits their practical applicability. To address this limitation, we propose VGIQA, a novel IQA model endowed with both local and global quality assessment capabilities. VGIQA is trained via a two-stage paradigm comprising a cold-start phase followed by reinforcement learning. Driven by carefully designed local quality assessment reward functions, our model exhibits exceptional image quality understanding on VGSR-2K, a new dataset we propose specifically for local quality evaluation, significantly outperforming existing open-source MLLMs such as Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct and Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct. Furthermore, the local quality evaluation task can effectively enhance the model's performance on the degradation perception task.

</details>


### [92] [3D-SceneQ: Empowering 3D LLM With Query-Guided Adaptive Pruning and Multi-Modal Feature Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37202)
*Yongqi Shan,Lu Zhang,Jiazuo Yu,Yunzhi Zhuge,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3D-SceneQ，将查询引导的自适应令牌剪枝与多模态语义增强统一到一个3D大语言模型中，显著提升3D场景理解、推理与定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D-LLM存在两点局限：一是拼接大量与任务无关的3D对象令牌，引入噪声，降低推理准确率并加剧幻觉；二是仅依赖点云，缺少2D模态中丰富的颜色、材质、纹理及高层上下文语义信息。

Method: 提出两个模块：(1) Query-Guided Adaptive Pruning (QGAP)，利用可学习的潜在查询，依据用户指令语义自适应筛选任务相关对象，保留关键信息并抑制无关噪声；(2) Multi-modal Object-level Feature Enhancement (MOFE)，将预训练2D模型的特征嵌入融合进3D表示，以丰富对象级语义信息。

Result: 在多个基准上优于当前最优方法，例如Scan2Cap上CIDEr提升5.7，SQA3D上EM@1提升17.0，在多种3D语言推理任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: 自适应令牌剪枝与多模态语义增强的结合可有效缓解噪声与语义匮乏问题，是提升3D-LLM场景理解与推理性能的有效范式。

Abstract: Current 3D scene understanding pipelines typically concatenate vast numbers of 3D object tokens with text tokens and feed the resulting sequence to a Large Language Model (LLM). However, existing 3D-LLMs for scene understanding encounter two major limitations: (i) excessive inclusion of task-irrelevant object data, introducing noise that reduces reasoning accuracy and increases hallucinations; and (ii) reliance solely on point cloud data, which inherently lacks the rich semantic information available in complementary 2D modalities, such as color, material properties, texture, and high-level contextual relationships. To address these challenges, we introduce 3D-SceneQ, a 3D LLM that unifies adaptive token pruning with multimodal semantic enrichment, markedly advancing scene understanding, reasoning, and grounding. Specifically, we propose a Query-Guided Adaptive Pruning (QGAP) module that adaptively selects task-relevant objects based on the intended meaning of user instructions, leveraging a learnable latent query to retain critical information while effectively suppressing irrelevant noise. In addition, we introduce a Multi-modal Object-level Feature Enhancement (MOFE) module, which integrates 2D feature embeddings from pre-trained models into 3D representation to enrich object-level semantic information. Evaluations show that 3D-SceneQ outperforms current state-of-the-art methods across multiple benchmarks (e.g., +5.7 CIDEr on Scan2Cap and +17.0 EM@1 on SQA3D), demonstrating its remarkable capabilities in various 3D language reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [93] [Uncovering Shortcut Amplification in Video RLVR through the Lens of Perceptual Uncertainty Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37211)
*Jiafeng Liang,Xianhao Ou,Tao Ren,Rongchuan Mu,Runxuan Liu,Dandan Tu,Ming Liu,See-Kiong Ng,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示视频RLVR存在"感知不确定性塌缩"与捷径放大问题，并提出双焦自蒸馏策略优化（BSPO）以强化视觉忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽提升LMM视频推理，但结果级优化可能放大捷径：模型偏向文本先验答案而非视觉证据，需从感知不确定性角度刻画该训练动力学并加以纠正。

Method: 通过对比视频条件与纯文本条件下的预测不确定性演化及梯度方向分析诊断问题；提出BSPO自蒸馏框架，利用组内正确/错误rollout提取经验推理原则以定位推理敏感token，并引入捷径感知的视觉扰动惩罚对视觉证据削弱时不变的预测，通过自适应重加权原始RL优势进行策略更新。

Result: 实验验证BSPO提升了视频推理性能，并增强了视觉忠实性（visual faithfulness）。

Conclusion: 结果级RLVR优化会持续锐化答案分布而削弱视觉证据带来的不确定性降低，偏向文本先验压缩；BSPO在保留RLVR任务收益的同时促使更新兼具推理敏感性与视觉根基性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as an effective post-training paradigm for enhancing the video reasoning capability of Large Multimodal Models (LMMs). Despite its empirical effectiveness, we show that outcome-level optimization can introduce an undesirable shortcut amplification effect, whereby models increasingly favor text-prior answers even when they are not supported by the visual evidence. To characterize this behavior, we investigate the training dynamics of video RLVR through the lens of perceptual uncertainty, analyzing how predictive uncertainty evolves under video-conditioned and text-only contexts. Our analysis identifies a perceptual uncertainty collapse phenomenon, in which RL training continuously sharpens the model's answer distribution while the uncertainty reduction attributable to visual evidence progressively diminishes. A further gradient-direction analysis reveals that such confidence enhancement becomes increasingly less aligned with visually grounded uncertainty reduction and is instead dominated by text-prior compression. Motivated by these findings, we propose Bifocal Self-distillation Policy Optimization (BSPO), a self-distillation framework that regulates policy updates with two complementary distributional signals. Specifically, BSPO derives empirical reasoning principles from intra-group correct and incorrect rollouts to identify reasoning-sensitive tokens, and introduces shortcut-aware visual perturbation to penalize predictions that remain invariant when task-relevant visual evidence is weakened. By adaptively reweighting the original RL advantage, BSPO preserves the task-solving benefits of RLVR while promoting updates that are both reasoning-sensitive and visually grounded. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of BSPO in improving video reasoning performance and strengthening visual faithfulness.

</details>


### [94] [Zhengrong-Spatial-Aware 1.0: Advancing 3D Multimodal Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37216)
*Yu Zhang,Xiaogang Wang,Yuhang Cheng,Jingrui Li,Wenbo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该报告实现了两条端到端多模态适配流水线用于3D感知视觉问答：一条基于带度量尺度点图的HiSpatial-3B微调，另一条基于QLoRA对Qwen3-VL（8B/32B）进行高效监督微调，并完成数据集转换、几何预处理、混合域训练、批量推理与自动评测的全栈工程，在两个空间基准上取得显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向3D感知的空间视觉问答需要几何感知能力，而通用多模态大模型在度量3D推理上表现差；同时缺乏可复现的、覆盖数据到评测的端到端适配工程方案，且专门化训练易损害通用多模态能力，故需建立可复现的适配范式并考察训练数据组成的影响。

Method: 构建两条流水线：(1) 微调HiSpatial-3B，将RGB观测与度量尺度3D点图（单目几何预处理）结合；(2) 以内存高效的QLoRA监督微调适配Qwen3-VL 8B与32B。完整工程栈包括数据集转换、图像-标注对齐、单目几何预处理、指令构建、混合域训练、检查点管理、批量推理与自动化评测。

Result: 目标空间基准上，HiSpatial适配使总体准确率由微调前1.73%提升至3,600步时的79.89%；在单独采集的风格化划分上由14.9%提升至55.1%，显示跨训练集迁移。Qwen3-VL流水线中，准确率在8B上由20.74%升至45.27%，在32B上由30.79%升至47.60%。

Conclusion: 两条互补且可复现的适配流水线分别面向几何感知与通用多模态空间推理，为后续度量3D推理、空间定位与领域适配多模态系统奠定工程基础；同时指出训练数据组成是专门化过程中保持通用多模态能力的关键因素。

Abstract: This report presents the implementation and validation of two end-to-end multimodal adaptation pipelines for 3D-aware visual question answering on a custom spatial dataset. The first pipeline fine-tunes HiSpatial-3B, which augments RGB observations with metric-scale 3D point maps; the second adapts Qwen3-VL through memory-efficient QLoRA-based supervised fine-tuning. The completed engineering stack covers dataset conversion, image-annotation alignment, monocular geometry preprocessing, instruction construction, mixed-domain training, checkpoint management, batch inference, and automated evaluation. On the target spatial benchmark, HiSpatial adaptation increases overall accuracy from 1.73% before fine-tuning to 79.89% at 3,600 steps. On a separately collected styled split, accuracy improves from 14.9% to 55.1%, indicating transfer beyond the original training set. The Qwen3-VL pipeline is likewise implemented end-to-end for both 8B- and 32B-scale models using mixed-domain QLoRA adaptation. The resulting accuracy improves from 20.74% to 45.27% for Qwen3-VL-8B and from 30.79% to 47.60% for Qwen3-VL-32B. Together, these results establish two complementary and reproducible adaptation pipelines for geometry-aware and general-purpose multimodal spatial reasoning. These results establish a reproducible engineering foundation for subsequent work on metric 3D reasoning, spatial grounding, and domain-adapted multimodal systems, while also identifying training-data composition as a central factor in preserving general multimodal capability during specialization.

</details>


### [95] [TopoField: Technical Report for ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37218)
*Zhiwei Chen,Zhiheng Fu,Zixu Li,Mingyu Zhang,Jiale Huang,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoField：基于VOLT的确定性"场到实例"解码器，用于室内点云功能部件分割，在ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D挑战赛中获第二名（AP 28.286%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能分割需定位室内点云中的小型交互部件（旋钮、按钮、插座）并输出带置信度排序的实例掩码。但逐点语义预测器产生的是连续概率场，而基准评估的是离散的带分实例集合，二者之间存在显著的"场→实例"鸿沟，需专门设计解码环节弥合。

Method: 将13次测试时前向传播聚合为语义共识场；增强阶段保留每个片段预测的处理点身份，在公共点数组中累加概率、按覆盖次数归一化并融合缓存；解码阶段分离高置信核心区与支撑区，构建对称的半径剪枝点图，用多源图测地分区拆分含多个种子的支撑区；最后进行类别感知的候选排序。

Result: 在30个验证场景、561个真值实例上，67.2%的实例存在IoU-0.5合格的候选，top-1覆盖率为20.0%；最终取得AP 28.286%、AP50 41.383%、AP25 47.558%。

Conclusion: 候选构建与候选排序构成互补瓶颈，需联合关注；该确定性解码框架可有效将语义概率场转化为高质量实例集合，并在挑战赛中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Functional segmentation requires locating small interactive parts, such as knobs, buttons, and sockets, in indoor point clouds and returning confidence-ranked instance masks. A point-wise semantic predictor produces a continuous probability field, whereas the benchmark evaluates a discrete set of scored instances. This creates a substantial field-to-instance gap. We present TopoField, a deterministic decoder built on top of VOLT. TopoField first converts 13 test-time forward passes into a semantic consensus field, then decodes instance candidates from the consensus geometry, and finally ranks the candidates in a class-aware manner. The augmentation stage retains the processed-point identity of every fragment prediction, accumulates probabilities in a common point array, normalizes by coverage count, and fuses the resulting caches. The decoding stage separates high-confidence cores from broader support regions, constructs a symmetric radius-pruned point graph, and uses a multi-source graph-geodesic partition to split support regions that contain multiple seeds. On 561 ground-truth instances across 30 validation scenes, an IoU-0.5-valid candidate exists for 67.2% of instances, and top-1 coverage is 20.0%. These complementary measurements motivate joint attention to candidate construction and ranking. TopoField achieves 28.286% AP, 41.383% AP50, and 47.558% AP25. TopoField achieved second place on the ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D Challenge.

</details>


### [96] [Vision-Language-Action or World-Action? Just Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37219)
*Yonghao Yu,Lang Huang,Runyi Li,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将VLA与WAM的差异解耦为骨干初始化、未来观测目标函数、推理阶段是否保留未来预测模块三项独立选择，提出统一框架Just Action Model（JAM）：预训练视觉骨干+动作专家，用动作损失与"前瞻损失"训练；前瞻损失以冻结编码器对未来观测的编码为目标、训练后丢弃预测头，故训练与推理均不涉及视觉生成，骨干可自由选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA与WAM阵营争论控制策略应继承视觉语言模型还是视觉生成器的预训练知识，但该争论把三个本可分离的因素混在一起，导致二者性能差异无法归因于单一因素。作者希望厘清究竟是哪一因素带来收益。

Method: - 解耦为：骨干初始化、作用于未来观测的目标、推理时是否保留未来损失所需机制；
- 统一为JAM：预训练视觉骨干 + 动作专家，联合优化动作损失与前瞻损失；
- 前瞻损失让当前观测特征回归记录未来观测的冻结编码，预测头训练后丢弃，无视觉生成、无额外推理开销；
- 实验设计：先在固定策略架构内比较目标函数，再跨骨干族验证前瞻配方。

Result: 在LIBERO与LIBERO-Plus上优于对应的FastWAM与ImageWAM；RoboTwin上成功率相当或略高。相对FastWAM，成功率由97.0%/55.3%/91.2%提升至98.2%/64.5%/91.4%。使用缓存前瞻目标时，每样本每次更新的在线训练FLOPs较FastWAM降低73%。

Conclusion: 未来监督可在不做视觉生成、不增加推理机制的前提下提升控制性能；且无论采用生成器初始化骨干还是视觉语言骨干，都能获得较强的前瞻表现。VLA与WAM的差距不应简单归因于骨干来源。

Abstract: The vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action model (WAM) communities debate whether control policies should inherit pretrained knowledge from vision-language models or from visual generators. The debate, however, bundles three separable choices: backbone initialization, the objective applied to future observations, and whether the machinery for that future loss is kept at inference. A gap between a VLA and a WAM therefore cannot simply be attributed to any one of them. We show that decoupling these choices yields a common framework for both families, which we term Just Action Model (JAM): a pretrained visual backbone with an action expert, trained with an action loss and a foresight loss. The foresight loss trains current-observation features to predict frozen encodings of the recorded future through a prediction head discarded after training, so no visual generation is involved in either training or inference, and the backbone can be chosen freely. We first compare objectives within fixed policy architectures, then evaluate the foresight recipe across backbone families. The resulting JAM policies outperform their matched FastWAM and ImageWAM counterparts on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, and achieve similar or slightly higher success rates on RoboTwin. Compared with FastWAM, JAM improves the success rates from 97.0%/55.3%/91.2% to 98.2%/64.5%/91.4%, respectively. With cached foresight targets, JAM reduces estimated online training FLOPs per sample per update by 73% relative to FastWAM. Our results show that future supervision improves control without visual generation or additional inference machinery, and strong foresight performance is attainable with either generator-initialized or vision-language backbones. Project Page: https://y0uroy.github.io/JAM

</details>


### [97] [Subspace Clifford Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37226)
*Zhongping Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Subspace Clifford Networks，用分组子空间交互替代全局循环滚动（cyclic rolling），将特征投影到划分为 G 个低维子空间（k∈{2,3,4}）的对齐空间，在每个 k 维组内计算全部楔积坐标并与 SiLU 激活的逐元素乘积拼接，组间由可学习 1×1 线性投影完成通道混合；同时给出融合 CUDA 核与重计算反向传播，并扩展为多分辨率层次化 Clifford 网络。CIFAR-100 上显著降低参数量与训练时间，层次化变体还提升分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 shift 的 Clifford 网络在整个 D 维通道空间上采样稀疏循环对角线，导致中间特征扩张大（约 10D）、参数与计算开销高。作者希望在不损失交互表达能力的前提下，用结构化分组子空间降低扩张与开销，并借小固定组尺寸实现高效 GPU 实现。

Method: 1）以 G 个低维组 V_g≅R^k（k∈{2,3,4}）构成对齐空间 R^{D̃}=⊕_{g}V_g，替代全局循环滚动；2）组内计算全部 C(k,2) 个楔积坐标，与 SiLU 激活后的逐元素乘积拼接；3）在分组交互前后夹入可学习 1×1 线性投影实现跨组通道混合；4）中间特征扩张从 2|S|D（5 shifts 时约 10D）降至 1.5D̃(k=2)/2.0D̃(k=3)/2.5D̃(k=4)；5）基于固定小 k 实现融合 CUDA 核，前向交互单核完成，反向采用重计算式解析梯度并使用线程局部变量，避免全局内存中间缓冲；6）骨干从各向同性柱状布局扩展为可配置逐阶段子空间维度的多分辨率 Hierarchical Clifford Network。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 上，所提模型在两种设定下均大幅降低参数量与训练时间；层次化变体在分类精度上优于所评估的 shift-based 层次化基线。

Conclusion: 分组子空间交互是全局循环滚动的有效替代：在减少特征扩张、参数与训练成本的同时保持乃至提升性能；小固定组尺寸利于融合核与无缓冲反向实现；多分辨率层次化扩展进一步带来精度收益。

Abstract: In this work, we propose Subspace Clifford Networks, a reformulation that replaces global cyclic rolling with grouped subspace interactions. Instead of sampling sparse cyclic diagonals across the entire $D$-dimensional channel space, we project the features into an aligned space $\R^{\tilde{D}} = \bigoplus_{g=1}^{G} V_g$ partitioned into $G$ low-dimensional groups $V_g \cong \R^k$ with $k \in \{2, 3, 4\}$. Within each $k$-dimensional group, we compute all $\binom{k}{2}$ wedge coordinates and concatenate them with $\operatorname{SiLU}$-activated coordinate-wise products, while sandwiching the grouped interaction between learnable $1\times 1$ linear projections to enable cross-group channel mixing. This design reduces intermediate feature expansion from $2|\mathcal{S}|D$ (typically $10D$ for 5 shifts) down to $1.5\tilde{D}$ ($k=2$), $2.0\tilde{D}$ ($k=3$), or $2.5\tilde{D}$ ($k=4$). Furthermore, exploiting the small, fixed group size $k \in \{2, 3, 4\}$, we implement a fused CUDA kernel that evaluates the forward interaction and a recomputation-based analytical backward pass using thread-local variables without allocating global-memory buffers for elementwise intermediates. Finally, we extend the backbone from an isotropic columnar layout to a multi-resolution Hierarchical Clifford Network with configurable per-stage subspace dimensions. Experiments on CIFAR-100 show that the proposed models substantially reduce parameter count and training time across both regimes, while the hierarchical variants also improve classification accuracy over the evaluated shift-based hierarchical baselines.

</details>


### [98] [HDVS: semi-supervised semantic segmentation via heterogeneous dual-branch voting supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37236)
*Yongqi Shan,Yunzhi Zhuge,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出异质双分支投票监督框架（HDVS），通过投票机制融合异质分支伪标签并引入特征级扰动，提升半监督语义分割伪标签可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督语义分割方法多基于"各分支生成的伪标签足够可靠、可用于监督其余分支"的假设，但协同训练中伪标签误差会累积，导致性能次优。

Method: 在交叉监督框架基础上：①引入投票机制，将来自异质分支的伪标签进行关联融合，生成可靠性更高的伪标签；②采用特征通信模块在各分支的特征层面引入扰动，在保持收敛的同时最大化双分支间预测多样性。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC 2012和Cityscapes两个基准数据集上全面评测，性能优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: HDVS通过提升伪标签可靠性与分支预测多样性，有效缓解伪标签误差累积问题，验证了异质双分支投票监督在半监督语义分割中的有效性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised semantic segmentation, which aims to utilize large volumes of unlabeled images to achieve accurate segmentation results with fewer human annotations, has attracted increasing attention. Prior methods were primarily based on the assumption that the pseudo-labels generated by each branch were sufficiently reliable to supervise the remaining branches. However, there is a concern that the errors in pseudo-labels could accumulate during co-training, potentially resulting in suboptimal performance. To this end, we present a novel framework called heterogeneous dual-branch voting supervision (HDVS), which is designed to enhance the reliability of pseudo-labels and mitigate the issues arising from pseudo-labeling. Specifically, based on the cross-supervision framework, we introduce a voting mechanism that correlates pseudo-labels from heterogeneous branches to produce pseudo-labels with enhanced reliability. Concurrently, we employ a feature communication module to introduce perturbations at the feature level in each branch to maximize prediction diversity across the dual branches while maintaining convergence. Comprehensive evaluations of the proposed HDVS on two benchmark datasets (PASCAL VOC 2012 and Cityscapes) demonstrate its superiority to the state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [99] [GameVerse: A Minute-Scale Gameplay Dataset for Long-Horizon Interactive World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37257)
*Kang He,Wenshuo Peng,Chuanhao Li,Zihui Gao,Xiaojie Xu,Zhengyuan Lin,Yuzhe Ding,Donghong Ji,Kaipeng Zhang,Yongtao Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GameVerse：分钟级游戏视频数据集（69.7K 片段、1,655 小时、3.496 亿帧），通过场景一致分割、质量过滤、相机位姿估计与事件级字幕标注的统一流程构建，用于长时程交互式世界建模，并显著延长了模型的rollout时间跨度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式世界模型在约一分钟rollout后难以保持一致性，其瓶颈不在模型容量，而在长时程训练数据稀缺：现有游戏视频数据集多为10秒以内短片，字幕以粗粒度、无区分的整片段描述，不足以支撑可控长时程生成。

Method: 构建 GameVerse 数据集，覆盖第一/第三人称游戏玩法，含 1,358 小时连续两分钟序列，比先前数据集长一个数量级；统一处理流程包括场景一致分割、质量过滤、相机位姿估计与基于事件的字幕标注；每条片段配有跨分钟时间稳定的相机轨迹，字幕将静态场景上下文与动态前景事件分离，并按事件边界（而非固定时间窗）对齐。

Result: 综合分析表明 GameVerse 在规模、多样性与标注质量上均优于现有公开数据集；实验证明在其上训练可显著延长交互式世界模型的时间跨度（temporal horizon）。

Conclusion: 长时程训练数据的稀缺是交互式世界模型一致性瓶颈的关键，GameVerse 作为面向长时程交互式世界建模的分钟级数据集，为可控长时程生成提供了数据基础，并有效提升模型的长时程一致性。

Abstract: Recent advances in interactive world models have made it possible to generate controllable, dynamic environments from action inputs. Yet current models struggle to maintain coherence beyond roughly one minute of rollout, a limitation rooted not in model capacity but in the scarcity of long-horizon training data. Existing gameplay video datasets typically consist of clips under ten seconds, with captions that describe whole clips in coarse and undifferentiated text, which is insufficient for controllable long-horizon generation. We address this with GameVerse, a minute-scale gameplay video dataset purpose-built for long-horizon interactive world modeling. GameVerse contains 69.7K clips spanning 1,655 hours and 349.6 million frames across diverse first- and third-person gameplay, including 1,358 hours of continuous two-minute sequences, an order of magnitude longer than prior datasets. Clips pass through a unified pipeline of scene-consistent segmentation, quality filtering, camera pose estimation, and event-based caption annotation. Each clip is paired with a temporally stable camera trajectory that remains coherent across minutes, together with captions that separate static scene context from dynamic foreground events and align to event boundaries rather than fixed time windows. Comprehensive analysis shows that GameVerse surpasses existing public datasets in scale, diversity, and annotation quality. Experiments further demonstrate that training on GameVerse substantially extends the temporal horizon of interactive world models.

</details>


### [100] [GradCO: Behavior-Aware Gradient-Conflict Offloading for Text-Based Person Anomaly Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37267)
*Zhiheng Fu,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Jiale Huang,Fangxu Liu,Jinhe Bi,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出GradCO框架解决文本行人异常检索（TPAS）中同身份异行为硬负样本导致的梯度冲突与外观线索淹没行为证据问题，含B-MoE、COL、HIA三模块，在五个基准上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TPAS需在保留身份线索的同时区分细粒度行为，存在两大挑战：(1)同身份、不同行为的硬负样本在细粒度对齐时引发梯度冲突；(2)外观线索强而行为证据弱，聚合时行为证据被淹没。现有文本行人检索方法未针对这些冲突设计。

Method: 提出GradCO（行为感知梯度冲突卸载），含三个互补模块：B-MoE构建外观流、正常行为流与异常行为流，其角色由检索目标端到端学习，无需专家级语义标签；COL在相似度计算中引入以配对为条件的卸载锚点，降低主文本表示上的冲突梯度负担；HIA以查询条件权重聚合各专家的相似度与特征。

Result: 在五个TPAS/TIPR基准上，GradCO显著优于现有SOTA方法，并通过实验验证了各模块设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过行为感知专家分流、冲突梯度卸载与层次化意图对齐，GradCO有效缓解梯度冲突和外观主导问题，为文本行人异常检索提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Text-based Person Anomaly Search (TPAS) retrieves a target pedestrian using natural-language descriptions of appearance and normal or anomalous behavior. Unlike conventional text-based person retrieval, TPAS must preserve identity cues while distinguishing fine-grained behaviors. It presents two challenges: same-identity, different-behavior hard negatives cause gradient conflict during granular alignment, while strong appearance cues can obscure weak behavioral evidence during aggregation. We propose GradCO (behavior-aware Gradient-Conflict Offloading), comprising three complementary components. Behavior-Aware Mixture-of-Experts (B-MoE) forms appearance, normal-behavior, and anomalous-behavior streams whose roles are learned from retrieval objectives without expert-specific semantic labels. Conflict Offload Learning (COL) uses pair-conditioned offload anchors in similarity computation to reduce the conflicting-gradient burden on the main text representations. Hierarchical Intent Alignment (HIA) aggregates expert similarities and features using query-conditioned weights. Experiments on five TPAS/TIPR benchmarks show GradCO significantly surpasses SOTA methods and validate our design.

</details>


### [101] [Taming Noise Initialization for Zero-shot Video Restoration with Image Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37290)
*Chong Wang,Zixuan Fu,Hao Cheng,Siyuan Yang,Alex C. Kot,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过DDIM反演从退化视频帧自身生成初始噪声，以保留内在时序相关性，并结合自适应噪声混合与测量引导轨迹注意力，实现零样本视频复原，在DAVIS、REDS4、Set8上优于现有零样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型预训练计算代价高昂，难以直接用于视频复原；现有方法借助预训练图像扩散模型，但采用帧间独立或相同高斯噪声初始化，无法捕捉并利用退化视频帧间的时序相关性。

Method: 1) 对受损视频帧本身执行DDIM反演生成初始噪声，以保留内在时序相关性；2) 提出自适应噪声混合策略，抑制DDIM反演中编码的退化模式并逐步将噪声引导至高斯分布；3) 提出测量引导的轨迹注意力（measurement-guided trajectory attention），增强跨帧特征对齐与时间一致性。

Result: 在多个视频复原任务上的大量实验表明，该方法在DAVIS、REDS4和Set8数据集上稳定优于现有零样本方法。

Conclusion: 合理的噪声初始化对零样本视频复原至关重要；利用退化帧反演获取带时序相关性的噪声，配合自适应混合与轨迹注意力，可在无需视频扩散预训练的情况下有效提升复原质量与时间一致性。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in restoring high-quality images in a zero-shot manner. Unfortunately, extending this success to video restoration is challenging due to the substantial computational resources required to pre-train video diffusion models. A rising trend seeks to address video restoration by utilizing a pre-trained image diffusion model, initiating restoration from either frame-wise independent or identical Gaussian noise. Yet, these approaches often fall short, as such simple noise initiations fail to capture and utilize the inherent temporal correlations within degraded video frames. In this work, we propose taming noise initialization for zero-shot video restoration, which preserves the intrinsic temporal correlations by generating initial noises directly using DDIM inversion on the corrupted video frames themselves. Building on this, we introduce an adaptive noise blending strategy that suppresses degradation patterns encoded during DDIM inversion while gradually steering the noise toward a Gaussian distribution. To further emphasize temporal consistency, we propose measurement-guided trajectory attention to better align features across frames. Extensive experiments on multiple video restoration tasks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing zero-shot approaches, consistently achieving superior results on the DAVIS, REDS4, and Set8 datasets.

</details>


### [102] [TRUCO: A Scalable Lock-Free Algorithm for Parallel Contour Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37297)
*Rafael Munoz-Salinas,Francisco J. Romero-Ramirez,Manuel J. Marin-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRUCO：一种无锁并行轮廓提取算法，通过起点所有权规则与推测式追踪实现高分辨率分割结果的高效几何边界提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将二值掩码转换为几何轮廓是计算机视觉流水线的关键后处理步骤，但Suzuki-Abe等标准算法本质串行、需构建拓扑层级，开销大；高分辨率场景下亟需利用多核CPU且避免分块拼接与繁重同步的方案。

Method: 提出TRUCO（Threaded Raster Unrestricted Contour Ownership）无锁并行算法：引入起点所有权规则（start-point ownership rule）界定轮廓归属，配合推测式追踪模型——线程向下追踪进入相邻区域的轮廓，并采用向上终止逻辑保证每条轮廓恰好被处理一次。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集、消费级与服务器级CPU上的实验表明，TRUCO显著优于现有基线，相较工业标准实现取得超过一个数量级的加速，并在多样硬件架构的高分辨率场景中保持良好可扩展性。

Conclusion: TRUCO通过所有权规则与推测式追踪消除了锁与分块拼接需求，在不构建拓扑层级的前提下实现了高并行、高可扩展的轮廓提取，适用于高分辨率分割后处理等实际流水线。

Abstract: Transforming binary masks into geometric contours remains a critical step in many computer vision pipelines, particularly for post-processing high-resolution segmentation outputs. Yet, standard algorithms such as the Suzuki-Abe method are limited by their sequential nature and the overhead of building topological hierarchies. As modern applications transition to high-resolution imaging, there is a critical need for contour extraction that leverages multi-core CPU architectures without the complexity of tile stitching or heavy synchronization.
  This paper presents TRUCO (Threaded Raster Unrestricted Contour Ownership), a lock-free parallel algorithm designed for high-performance geometric boundary extraction. TRUCO introduces a start-point ownership rule, combined with a speculative tracing model, in which threads trace contours downward into adjacent regions and use an upward-termination logic to ensure each contour is processed exactly once.
  Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets, conducted on both consumer and server-grade CPUs, demonstrate that TRUCO significantly outperforms existing baselines. The method achieves speedups of over an order of magnitude compared to the industry standard while maintaining robust scalability on high-resolution scenes across diverse hardware architectures.

</details>


### [103] [MvT: Event-based Multi-view Projection for Multiple Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37309)
*Muxi Zha,Banglei Guan,Minzu Liang,Zibin Liu,Yang Shang,Qifeng Yu,Laurent Kneip*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多视角跟踪流水线MvT，在保留2D伪帧格式的同时引入额外时空视角，以解决事件相机单视角跟踪中的歧义问题，且无需训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机用于多目标跟踪时，主流伪帧范式虽可直接复用图像技术，但当轨迹重叠或空间邻近时会出现跟踪失败，即单视角歧义问题。

Method: MvT包含多视角投影（MvP）与多视角融合（MvF）两个模块。MvP将事件编码为三个互补的时空视角并缓解模式离散化；MvF将多视角结果统一到3D坐标系，并通过带特定准则组合的优化模型进行轨迹关联。

Result: 在包括自采集小目标数据集（SOD）在内的四个数据集上，MvT可无缝集成图像方法，在泛化场景下优于现有非学习与学习型跟踪器，并有效解决单视角跟踪歧义。

Conclusion: MvT为免训练方法，适用于真值标注不可行的场景，具备实用性与数据高效潜力。

Abstract: Event cameras are increasingly used for Multiple Object Tracking (MOT), but their asynchronous event output often requires specialized methods. Existing processing methods primarily follow two paradigms, pseudo-frames and event-by-event. The former is the prevailing approach since its data format aligns with images, making image-based techniques applicable. However, it suffers from tracking failures when trajectories overlap or are spatially close on pseudo-frames. Facing this challenge, we propose a multi-view pipeline, Multi-view Tracking (MvT), which preserves the 2D data format to leverage image-based techniques directly while introducing additional spatio-temporal views to resolve tracking ambiguities in a single view. MvT comprises a Multi-view Projection (MvP) module and a Multi-view Fusion (MvF) stage. MvP encodes events into three complementary spatio-temporal views while mitigating the pattern discretization. Within MvF, multi-view results are unified into a 3D coordinate system, and tracklets are associated through an optimization model subject to specific criteria combination. Evaluations on four datasets, including our self-collected Small Objects Dataset (SOD), show that MvT seamlessly integrates image-based methods and outperforms existing non-learning and learning trackers in generalized scenarios, and effectively resolves the single-view tracking ambiguities. Being training-free, MvT is applicable when ground-truth annotation is infeasible, thereby highlighting its practical, data-efficient potential. Code is available at https://github.com/zhazhabiu/MvTracking.

</details>


### [104] [A Lightweight Attention-Based CNN with Saliency-Map Interpretability for Skin Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37314)
*Sanjana Islam Kasfia,Md. Ismiel Hossen Abir,AKM Bahalul Haque,Abir Mahmud Shahariar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级注意力CNN（CNN-Attention）用于皮肤病变二分类，以87%准确率、195万参数、7.45MB存储达到与MobileNet相当的精度，并结合显著图实现可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤癌高发且威胁生命，早期准确诊断可显著提升生存率；但皮损在视觉上高度相似，人工皮肤镜评估困难，同时需要兼顾计算成本与模型可解释性。

Method: 构建含通道注意力的轻量CNN-Attention模型，聚焦皮肤镜图像中的信息区域并保持低计算复杂度；使用公开数据集（训练2637张、测试660张）并采用平衡类别权重；以ROC AUC、PR AUC评估，并用显著图（saliency map）可视化决策相关区域；与VGG16、VGG19、ResNet101、MobileNet对比。

Result: 准确率87%，参数量195万、存储7.45MB，性能与最佳基线MobileNet持平，而参数与存储约为其一半；ROC AUC 0.9475，PR AUC 0.9430。

Conclusion: 该框架在分类性能、计算效率与可解释性之间取得良好平衡，验证了轻量通道注意力结构与显著图解释在皮肤病变自动诊断中的有效性。

Abstract: Skin cancer is among the most prevalent and life threatening forms of cancer worldwide. Early and accurate diagnosis significantly improves patient survival rates. However, manual dermoscopic assessment remains challenging due to visual similarity. This study proposes an Explainable AI-driven framework for automated binary classification of skin lesions using a lightweight attention-based convolutional neural network (CNN-Attention). The model integrates channel attention to focus on informative regions of dermoscopic images while maintaining low computational complexity. A publicly available dataset comprising 2,637 training and 660 test images was used, with balanced class weights. The proposed model achieved 87 percent accuracy with 1.95 million parameters and 7.45 MB of storage, matching the best baseline (MobileNet) while using approximately half as many parameters and half as much storage. ROC AUC and PR AUC scores reached 0.9475 and 0.9430, respectively. Additionally, saliency maps were incorporated as an explainability technique to visualize regions that influence model decisions. Comparative analysis with VGG16,VGG19,ResNet101,and MobileNet demonstrates the proposed models effectiveness in balancing classification results, computational efficiency, and interpretability.

</details>


### [105] [Hierarchical Sparse Graph for Multi-dimensional Assignment in Event-based Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37321)
*Muxi Zha,Yang Shang,Wenlong Zhang,Qifeng Yu,Banglei Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于模糊逻辑与层次稀疏图的事件相机多批次轨迹关联方法，解决事件检测器"一对多"导致的欠定多对多匹配问题，无需训练即在eTram与Ev-UAV上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机跟踪多为在线式、仅关联相邻批次，时间窗口过窄，在长期遮挡与漏检场景易失败；需引入更广时间上下文。而事件检测器对单目标在一批次内可产生多个空间上不同但均正确的轨迹片段，违反经典数据关联的一对一约束，使关联成为欠定多对多匹配的NP难问题。

Method: 聚焦跨多批次的事件式多维分配。以模糊逻辑形式化分配问题，用多个长程运动学度量量化轨迹片段两两相关性，无需硬阈值即可融合异构度量；将轨迹片段视为节点、模糊相关性视为边，构建层次稀疏图（HSG），把分配转化为图划分任务，并以启发式方法依据顶点亲和度自适应划分；全流程无需训练。

Result: 在eTram与Ev-UAV两个挑战性事件数据集上，方法优于现有事件式及多数图像式关联模型；在多对多关联场景中各项指标达OC-SORT的2倍以上。

Conclusion: 将模糊相关性与层次稀疏图划分相结合，可在无训练条件下有效求解事件式欠定多对多关联，验证了多批次全局时间上下文对高精度跟踪的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: Existing event-based tracking methods typically operate in an online manner, associating tracklets across adjacent batches. However, this limited temporal window often leads to failures in challenging scenarios, such as long-term occlusions or missed detections, where broader temporal context should be incorporated for high-precision tracking. To achieve robust and accurate tracking, this paper concentrates on the event-based multi-dimensional assignment that associates tracklets across multiple batches simultaneously. Unlike image-based detectors, event-based detectors can generate multiple correct yet spatially distinct tracklets for a single object per batch. This violates the one-to-one rule in classic data association, making event-based association an underdetermined many-to-many matching problem. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to tackle this NP-hard problem.
